PROBLEM
------------------------------
You are visiting San Francisco for the day and want to meet as many friends as possible. Solve the problem by considering various different schedules and picking the best one to optimize your goals.

Travel distances (in minutes):
Marina District to Mission District: 20.
Mission District to Marina District: 19.

CONSTRAINTS: You arrive at Marina District at 9:00AM. Stephanie will be at Mission District from 10:30AM to 1:30PM. You'd like to meet Stephanie for a minimum of 120 minutes.

Your response should start with 'SOLUTION:'.
================================================================================


ENVIRONMENT
------------------------------
You are visiting San Francisco for the day and want to meet as many friends as possible. Solve the problem by considering various different schedules and picking the best one to optimize your goals.

Travel distances (in minutes):
Marina District to Mission District: 20.
Mission District to Marina District: 19.

CONSTRAINTS: You arrive at Marina District at 9:00AM. Stephanie will be at Mission District from 10:30AM to 1:30PM. You'd like to meet Stephanie for a minimum of 120 minutes.

Your response should start with 'SOLUTION:'.
================================================================================


FINAL-PLAN
------------------------------
<domain>
(define (domain san_francisco_visit)
  (:requirements :strips :typing)
  (:types
    location
    time
    count
    person
  )
  (:predicates
    (at ?l - location)
    (time_at ?t - time)
    (meeting_progress ?p - person ?c - count)
    (stephanie_at ?p - person ?l - location ?t - time)
    (next_time ?t1 ?t2 - time)
    (next_count ?c1 ?c2 - count)
    (travel_duration_marina_mission ?t1 ?t2 - time)
    (travel_duration_mission_marina ?t1 ?t2 - time)
  )

  (:action wait
    :parameters (?l - location ?t_now ?t_next - time)
    :precondition (and
      (at ?l)
      (time_at ?t_now)
      (next_time ?t_now ?t_next)
    )
    :effect (and
      (not (time_at ?t_now))
      (time_at ?t_next)
    )
  )

  (:action travel_marina_to_mission
    :parameters (?t_now ?t_later - time)
    :precondition (and
      (at marina)
      (time_at ?t_now)
      (travel_duration_marina_mission ?t_now ?t_later)
    )
    :effect (and
      (not (at marina))
      (at mission)
      (not (time_at ?t_now))
      (time_at ?t_later)
    )
  )

  (:action travel_mission_to_marina
    :parameters (?t_now ?t_later - time)
    :precondition (and
      (at mission)
      (time_at ?t_now)
      (travel_duration_mission_marina ?t_now ?t_later)
    )
    :effect (and
      (not (at mission))
      (at marina)
      (not (time_at ?t_now))
      (time_at ?t_later)
    )
  )

  (:action talk_to_stephanie
    :parameters (?p - person ?t_now ?t_next - time ?c_now ?c_next - count)
    :precondition (and
      (at mission)
      (time_at ?t_now)
      (next_time ?t_now ?t_next)
      (stephanie_at ?p mission ?t_now)
      (meeting_progress ?p ?c_now)
      (next_count ?c_now ?c_next)
    )
    :effect (and
      (not (time_at ?t_now))
      (time_at ?t_next)
      (not (meeting_progress ?p ?c_now))
      (meeting_progress ?p ?c_next)
    )
  )
)
</domain>
<problem>

================================================================================


DOMAIN
------------------------------
(define (domain san_francisco_visit)
  (:requirements :strips :typing)
  (:types
    location
    time
    count
    person
  )
  (:predicates
    (at ?l - location)
    (time_at ?t - time)
    (meeting_progress ?p - person ?c - count)
    (stephanie_at ?p - person ?l - location ?t - time)
    (next_time ?t1 ?t2 - time)
    (next_count ?c1 ?c2 - count)
    (travel_duration_marina_mission ?t1 ?t2 - time)
    (travel_duration_mission_marina ?t1 ?t2 - time)
  )

  (:action wait
    :parameters (?l - location ?t_now ?t_next - time)
    :precondition (and
      (at ?l)
      (time_at ?t_now)
      (next_time ?t_now ?t_next)
    )
    :effect (and
      (not (time_at ?t_now))
      (time_at ?t_next)
    )
  )

  (:action travel_marina_to_mission
    :parameters (?t_now ?t_later - time)
    :precondition (and
      (at marina)
      (time_at ?t_now)
      (travel_duration_marina_mission ?t_now ?t_later)
    )
    :effect (and
      (not (at marina))
      (at mission)
      (not (time_at ?t_now))
      (time_at ?t_later)
    )
  )

  (:action travel_mission_to_marina
    :parameters (?t_now ?t_later - time)
    :precondition (and
      (at mission)
      (time_at ?t_now)
      (travel_duration_mission_marina ?t_now ?t_later)
    )
    :effect (and
      (not (at mission))
      (at marina)
      (not (time_at ?t_now))
      (time_at ?t_later)
    )
  )

  (:action talk_to_stephanie
    :parameters (?p - person ?t_now ?t_next - time ?c_now ?c_next - count)
    :precondition (and
      (at mission)
      (time_at ?t_now)
      (next_time ?t_now ?t_next)
      (stephanie_at ?p mission ?t_now)
      (meeting_progress ?p ?c_now)
      (next_count ?c_now ?c_next)
    )
    :effect (and
      (not (time_at ?t_now))
      (time_at ?t_next)
      (not (meeting_progress ?p ?c_now))
      (meeting_progress ?p ?c_next)
    )
  )
)
================================================================================

------------------------------

NATURAL-PLAN sas_plan_1
------------------------------
SOLUTION:

To meet Betty in San Francisco, follow these steps:

1. **Travel to the Financial District:** Depart from Richmond District at 9:00 AM. The trip takes 22 minutes, arriving at the Financial District at 9:22 AM.
2. **Wait for the meeting window:** Wait at the Financial District from 9:22 AM until 5:15 PM (17:15), which is when Betty becomes available.
3. **Meet with Betty:** Meet Betty at the Financial District for the required 60 minutes, starting at 5:15 PM and concluding at 6:15 PM (18:15).

NATURAL-PLAN sas_plan_1
------------------------------
To meet Kenneth for the required 90 minutes, follow this schedule:

1.  **Wait at Fisherman's Wharf:** Remain at Fisherman's Wharf from your arrival at 9:00 AM until 2:04 PM.
2.  **Travel to Nob Hill:** At 2:04 PM, depart Fisherman's Wharf and travel to Nob Hill. You will arrive at 2:15 PM (an 11-minute trip).
3.  **Begin Meeting:** Upon arrival at Nob Hill at 2:15 PM, meet Kenneth to start your session.
4.  **Conduct Meeting:** Stay at Nob Hill with Kenneth from 2:15 PM until 3:45 PM.
5.  **Complete Meeting:** Conclude the meeting at 3:45 PM. This satisfies the requirement for a 90-minute meeting with Kenneth.

NATURAL-PLAN sas_plan_1
------------------------------
To meet your goal of meeting John in San Francisco, follow these steps:

1.  **Travel from Richmond District to North Beach:** Depart from Richmond District at 9:00 AM and travel to North Beach. This journey takes 17 minutes, arriving at 9:17 AM.
2.  **Wait at North Beach:** Since John does not arrive until the afternoon, wait at North Beach from 9:17 AM until 3:15 PM.
3.  **Meet with John:** Meet John at North Beach starting at 3:15 PM and ending at 4:30 PM. This satisfies the requirement of meeting him for 75 minutes during his window of availability.

**SOLUTION:** Your schedule is complete, and you have successfully met John at North Beach.

CONSTRAINTS: You arrive at Chinatown at 9:00AM. Stephanie will be at Marina District from 8:00AM to 3:00PM. You'd like to meet Stephanie for a minimum of 105 minutes.

NATURAL-PLAN sas_plan_1
------------------------------
SOLUTION:

To meet Mary for at least 75 minutes at Nob Hill, follow this schedule:

1.  **Travel to Nob Hill**: Depart from Union Square at 9:00 AM. You will arrive at Nob Hill at 9:09 AM (a 9-minute trip).
2.  **Wait for the meeting**: Stay at Nob Hill from 9:09 AM until 12:00 PM, which is when Mary becomes available.
3.  **Meet with Mary**: Meet Mary at Nob Hill starting at 12:00 PM and conclude the meeting at 1:15 PM. This fulfills the requirement of meeting for exactly 75 minutes.

NATURAL-PLAN sas_plan_1
------------------------------
SOLUTION:

To meet with James as planned, follow these steps:

1.  **Travel to the Marina District**: Depart from Golden Gate Park at 9:00 AM and travel to the Marina District. You will arrive at 9:16 AM (a 16-minute trip).
2.  **Wait for James**: Once you arrive at the Marina District at 9:16 AM, wait there until 10:15 AM, which is when James is scheduled to arrive.
3.  **Meet with James**: Meet James at the Marina District starting at 10:15 AM. Conduct the meeting for 15 minutes, concluding at 10:30 AM.

NATURAL-PLAN sas_plan_2

SOLUTION:

To meet your goal of meeting Betty in San Francisco, follow this schedule:

1. **Travel to North Beach**: Depart from the Presidio at 9:00 AM. The trip to North Beach takes 18 minutes, so you will arrive at 9:18 AM.
2. **Wait**: Stay at North Beach and wait from 9:18 AM until 6:45 PM, which is when Betty becomes available.
3. **Meet Betty**: Meet with Betty at North Beach starting at 6:45 PM. Continue the meeting until 8:00 PM to fulfill the required 75-minute duration.

------------------------------
To meet Mary for the required 120 minutes at the Marina District, follow these steps:

1.  **Travel to the Marina District:** Depart from Nob Hill at 9:00 AM and travel to the Marina District. You will arrive at 9:11 AM (an 11-minute trip).
2.  **Wait for the Meeting:** Once at the Marina District, wait from 9:11 AM until 8:00 PM for Mary to become available.
3.  **Meet with Mary:** Meet Mary at the Marina District starting at 8:00 PM. The meeting will last for 120 minutes, concluding at 10:00 PM.
